## Development

- The main repo lives on Gitea (`privat/ha-wireguard-client`), where Renovate
  keeps dependencies current: everything but major updates merges itself, and
  every update under `wireguard_client/` bumps the app version. A push mirror
  copies `main` to GitHub.
- On GitHub, CI lints, runs the end-to-end test and, when `config.yaml` carries
  a version that is not on ghcr yet, builds and publishes
  `ghcr.io/dergary/ha-wireguard-client` for amd64 and aarch64.
- `test/e2e.sh` runs the app against a real WireGuard server container and a
  mock Supervisor (Docker with WireGuard kernel support, about 9 minutes).
- `.devcontainer.json` starts a full Supervisor + Home Assistant with this repo
  as local app repository.

  every update under `wireguard_client/` bumps the app version. A push mirror
  copies `main` to GitHub.
- On GitHub, CI lints, runs the end-to-end test and, when `config.yaml` carries
  a version that is not on ghcr yet, builds and publishes
  `ghcr.io/dergary/ha-wireguard-client` for amd64 and aarch64.
- `test/e2e.sh` runs the app against a real WireGuard server container and a
  mock Supervisor (Docker with WireGuard kernel support, about 9 minutes).
- `.devcontainer.json` starts a full Supervisor + Home Assistant with this repo
  as local app repository.

  keeps dependencies current: everything but major updates merges itself, and
  every update under `wireguard_client/` bumps the app version. A push mirror
  copies `main` to GitHub.
- On GitHub, CI lints, runs the end-to-end test and, when `config.yaml` carries
  a version that is not on ghcr yet, builds and publishes
  `ghcr.io/dergary/ha-wireguard-client` for amd64 and aarch64.
- `test/e2e.sh` runs the app against a real WireGuard server container and a
  mock Supervisor (Docker with WireGuard kernel support, about 9 minutes).
- `.devcontainer.json` starts a full Supervisor + Home Assistant with this repo
  as local app repository.

  keeps dependencies current: everything but major updates merges itself, and
  every update under `wireguard_client/` bumps the app version. A push mirror
  copies `main` to GitHub.
- On GitHub, CI lints, runs the end-to-end test and, when `config.yaml` carries
  a version that is not on ghcr yet, builds and publishes
  `ghcr.io/dergary/ha-wireguard-client` for amd64 and aarch64.
- `test/e2e.sh` runs the app against a real WireGuard server container and a
  mock Supervisor (Docker with WireGuard kernel support, about 9 minutes).
- `.devcontainer.json` starts a full Supervisor + Home Assistant with this repo
  as local app repository.

  every update under `wireguard_client/` bumps the app version. A push mirror
  copies `main` to GitHub.
- On GitHub, CI lints, runs the end-to-end test and, when `config.yaml` carries
  a version that is not on ghcr yet, builds and publishes
  `ghcr.io/dergary/ha-wireguard-client` for amd64 and aarch64.
- `test/e2e.sh` runs the app against a real WireGuard server container and a
  mock Supervisor (Docker with WireGuard kernel support, about 9 minutes).
- `.devcontainer.json` starts a full Supervisor + Home Assistant with this repo
  as local app repository.

- The main repo lives on Gitea (`privat/ha-wireguard-client`), where Renovate
  keeps dependencies current: everything but major updates merges itself, and
  every update under `wireguard_client/` bumps the app version. A push mirror
  copies `main` to GitHub.
- On GitHub, CI lints, runs the end-to-end test and, when `config.yaml` carries
  a version that is not on ghcr yet, builds and publishes
  `ghcr.io/dergary/ha-wireguard-client` for amd64 and aarch64.
- `test/e2e.sh` runs the app against a real WireGuard server container and a
  mock Supervisor (Docker with WireGuard kernel support, about 9 minutes).
- `.devcontainer.json` starts a full Supervisor + Home Assistant with this repo
  as local app repository.

- The main repo lives on Gitea (`privat/ha-wireguard-client`), where Renovate
  keeps dependencies current: everything but major updates merges itself, and
  every update under `wireguard_client/` bumps the app version. A push mirror
  copies `main` to GitHub.
- On GitHub, CI lints, runs the end-to-end test and, when `config.yaml` carries
  a version that is not on ghcr yet, builds and publishes
  `ghcr.io/dergary/ha-wireguard-client` for amd64 and aarch64.
- `test/e2e.sh` runs the app against a real WireGuard server container and a
  mock Supervisor (Docker with WireGuard kernel support, about 9 minutes).
- `.devcontainer.json` starts a full Supervisor + Home Assistant with this repo
  as local app repository.

- The main repo lives on Gitea (`privat/ha-wireguard-client`), where Renovate
  keeps dependencies current: everything but major updates merges itself, and
  every update under `wireguard_client/` bumps the app version. A push mirror
  copies `main` to GitHub.
- On GitHub, CI lints, runs the end-to-end test and, when `config.yaml` carries
  a version that is not on ghcr yet, builds and publishes
  `ghcr.io/dergary/ha-wireguard-client` for amd64 and aarch64.
- `test/e2e.sh` runs the app against a real WireGuard server container and a
  mock Supervisor (Docker with WireGuard kernel support, about 9 minutes).
- `.devcontainer.json` starts a full Supervisor + Home Assistant with this repo
  as local app repository.
